// XDK symbols in retail sf33rd.xbe.
// These are static-library symbols, not game names. They are not in funcs.txt.
// 405 come from XbSymbolDatabase OOVPA signatures (XbSymbolDatabaseCLI -e).
// The database is not part of this tree.
// QueryPerformanceFrequency, XGetVideoFlags, XGetAVPack,
// XGetVideoStandard, XCalculateSignatureUpdate, and
// XCalculateSignatureEnd were named from their bodies. That scan left them unnamed.
// 85 more were named from their bodies: 35 XAPILIB wrappers (file,
// heap, thread, string, saved-game, and setting calls), identified by the
// kernel imports they call, their strings, and their argument counts;
// 45 C and C++ runtime functions (lib:LIBCMT); four D3DX8 routines; and
// Direct3DCreate8, which returns a dummy non-null value.
// _EH_prolog and _aullshr take their arguments in registers. They are
// listed as cdecl.
// The rest were matched against the XDK 5849 static libraries (xapilib,
// libcmt, d3d8, d3dx8, dsound, xacteng, xnet(s), xonline(s), xvoice, and
// others). Each library function's bytes, with relocated bytes masked,
// matched exactly one place. Its kernel imports had to name the same
// kernel functions, and its calls had to land where their callees
// resolved. Callees reached that way are named too. A match of fewer
// than 16 fixed bytes needs one confirmed call or import. Bodies that
// several functions share, and names inside game code, were left out,
// except the D3DDevice header wrappers (D3DDevice__Name, the C++ inline
// that calls D3DDevice_Name) and D3DX's private zlib deflate code. lib is
// the library the object came from. C++ names are scope_..._method.
// The SDK is not part of this tree.
//
// kind is func or var. conv is the detected calling convention.
// calls is a direct call or jmp in .text whose target is this address.
// The count walks each scanned .text span and stops at the first byte
// that does not decode, so a call after a jump table is not included.
// prefix is the first 8 bytes when that address is in the file.
// bss:1 is inside a section and past that section's file bytes.
// The offset list is struct fields, not virtual addresses.
//
